## Execution Mode

- **direct**: Execute units sequentially in the coordinating task. Fresh sub-agents perform the mandatory hostile reviews. Before protected handoff, this task may create and protect only `spoonjoy-delivery` plus dedicated empty delivery-control-plane GitHub App and Cloudflare broker namespaces; it cannot target or consume source-owned web/native, production Cloudflare, ASC, TestFlight, notification, build, secret, or worktree state. Source-repository work begins only after the retained TestFlight task's protected handoff validates.
- **public evidence**: Every repository artifact/output uses repository, random 128-bit stable worktree ID, role, branch, and base SHA only. No local path or unkeyed path-derived digest is public. ID-to-path mappings and optional keyed HMAC integrity commitments exist solely in private Desk cleanup state and are never committed, uploaded, logged, summarized, or embedded through another object. Until Unit 6c is complete, every command transcript and hostile fixture result remains outside the repository in `0600` private ephemeral storage; Unit 1a, Unit 1b, Unit 1c, Unit 2a, Unit 2b, Unit 2c, Unit 3a, Unit 3b, Unit 3c, Unit 4a, Unit 4b, Unit 4c, Unit 5a, Unit 5b, and Unit 5c commit code/tests only and record private locators plus SHA-256 digests in Desk. Unit 6c scans those retained bytes and may then commit only allowlisted summaries/digests; raw bytes are purged after the backfill proof.

## Objective

Build and pilot a production-grade delivery system that carries one Spoonjoy product contract through web/backend, native Apple, and MCP/agent implementations to independently verified exact releases and terminal cleanup. Only a protected `ReleaseSetPublished` append with its mandatory exact post-result `PublicationObserved` proof may declare the Product Change shipped.

- [ ] A one-time audited `ledger-root-bootstrap-v1` ceremony installs distinct least-privilege Ledger Writer and Delivery Guardian Apps, anchors distinct workflow-inaccessible reviewer-attestation and governance-observer keys, installs `ProtectedReleaseTagV1`, and permanently disables setup after Ledger Writer appends `RootAuthorityInstalled`. Ledger Writer is the sole `release-ledger` and canonical release-tag-namespace bypass actor with delivery `contents:write` plus read-only Actions/Checks provenance permissions; its tag broker exposes exact create-if-absent tag and body-only empty-assets Release creation only, with no update/delete/upload route or implicit-tag branch. A root-authorized local three-GET-only observer signs actor/admin/full-bypass/ETag one-use ruleset snapshots before/after tag creation without granting Administration to Ledger Writer. Guardian is zero-bypass and sole exact-provenance required-check actor. The root authorizes one separate journaled `source-guardian-cutover-v1`; handoff requires its exact `SourceGuardianCutoverCompleted` terminal. App keys/tokens remain outside Actions in purpose-specific OIDC brokers; same-Actions-App check spoofing, forged/replayed reviewer envelopes, identity substitution, ordinary `GITHUB_TOKEN`, other Apps, direct, and self-declared writes/checks fail.
- [ ] Static typed operation DAGs authorize all real web/native/governance/provider alternatives; runtime receipts bind authoritative pre-state, hash-linked resolved inputs, selected branch, sanitized result, retries, and containment.
- [ ] Production deploy, migrations/capabilities/rollback/canary/reporting, TestFlight upload/metadata/group/notification/expiry, cleanup, artifact/attestation, finalization, projection, and tag paths reject missing, stale, revoked, wrong-SHA/generation/graph/claim authorization.
- [ ] Source-owned attestors use exact reviewed delivery SHA and purpose-specific brokers: ASC reads are GET-only with a dedicated read credential; mutations use a distinct preferred app-limited or explicitly scoped-fallback credential, a per-run claim-bound X25519 recipient, and an exact-operation local broker. Provider keys/JWTs/generic leases never enter Actions or untrusted children. The Transporter TCB is the isolated broker plus the runtime-observed complete code-signed launcher/app/JVM/helper/JAR/library/descendant executable closure with pinned signatures/tree digests, sterile broker-owned `HOME`/`CWD`/`TMPDIR`, one exact private key path, and all alternate searched key locations proven absent or sandbox-inaccessible. Raw output stays private; sanitized attestations are dispatched/verified through authenticated `gh`.
- [ ] Every evidence class passes public/private/forbidden classification and adversarial leak scanning before logs, summaries, annotations, caches, artifacts, commits, or releases.
- [ ] Rebaseline validates exact web/native main SHAs, release-task commit, protected handoff/acknowledgment, zero in-flight source mutations, and cleanup ownership before source edits.
- [ ] Current and previous client identities freeze; previous-source debug/simulator and cleanup plus independent attestation and previous-installed queue seed are direct same-generation predecessors of production promotion. Exact source config pins `preview_urls:false` and `workers_dev:false`; a closed authoritative `WorkerInvocationSurfaceV1` covers deployment/traffic/overrides, versioned and aliased previews, `workers.dev`, routes, custom domains, service bindings, dispatch namespaces, Durable Objects, queue/scheduled/email/tail/workflow triggers, and unknown provider kinds. A provider-enforced `WorkerOverrideBarrierV1` blocks the version-override header on every production route/custom domain, permits it only through one temporary Access-protected staging hostname with a claim-bound broker-held provider token plus one-use operation grants, and proves every service-binding caller strips or denies the header. The exact attested version promotes or Unit 51 immediately restores the previous 100% deployment and proves the candidate unreachable through every external/internal invocation surface; only then can the queued previous-installed mutation replay idempotently. Pre-provenance binary attestation is explicit.

- [ ] Desk/repository records contain exact source SHAs, three digests, provider attestations, Worker/native/ASC identities, installed proof, cleanup proof, and authoritative ledger commit.

## Code Coverage Requirements

**MANDATORY: 100% coverage on all new code.**
- No coverage exclusions or warning suppression on new delivery, web, workflow-support, or native core logic.
- Every statement, branch, function, line, error path, retry, parser failure, transition, race, predicate alternative, redaction, provider failure, rollback, supersession, and cleanup refusal is tested.
- Edge cases include null, empty, duplicate, Unicode, numeric boundary, expiry boundary, malformed SHA/digest/ID, unknown field/type, ambiguous predicate, stale read, concurrent append, partial provider failure, and leaked evidence.
- Full repository suites and changed-code coverage remain green with zero warnings before each merge.

## TDD Requirements

**Strict TDD - no exceptions:**
1. **Tests first**: Write failing tests before implementation.
2. **Verify failure**: Run the focused command and capture the expected red result in private `0600` ephemeral storage. Before Unit 6c, commit no command transcript or fixture output; after Unit 6c, publish only scanner-approved summaries/digests.
3. **Minimal implementation**: Write only enough code to satisfy the contract.
4. **Verify pass**: Re-run focused tests and capture green evidence under the same private-before-Unit-6c/public-after-scan rule.
5. **Refactor**: Keep behavior and coverage green.
6. **No skipping**: Workflow, settings, shell, Swift, and live-provider behavior require executable contract tests, not prose assertions.

**Finalization relay schema implementation**: Implement timing/lease policy, exact protected-ledger-ref GitHub-Date records bound to each attempt's journaled expected head, barrier receipts, nonrenewable `validatorLeaseDurationMs=120000` fenced attempts, and one no-egress Durable Object validation transaction over its own sealed bytes. Transaction rollback leaves `unvalidated`; committed validation is idempotently observable; exact fence Date equality succeeds and one millisecond early fails. First dispatch accepts only immutable validation; a separately implemented read-only fallback can release only digest-equal bytes after `dispatch_committed`. Implement retrieval-only zero-storage-write repair, other repair activation, and terminal teardown; effective observation requires operation capability nonreachability. Frozen semantics/package/source/candidate/DO transition code/abort/governance remain forbidden.
**Replay execution schema implementation**: Implement only the enumerated pass/node/cursor transitions plus root-derived lease arithmetic. `ReplayPassGenesisV1` is the sole absent-pointer transition: for the exact root-authorized Product Change with zero pass history, one CAS creates p1 and its complete unclaimed node map, installs cursor `at(firstNodeID)` and the active pointer, and changes only `staged -> open`; exact queried equality reconciles response loss, while any mismatch or prior history fails. Later activation atomically changes the prior `resolved | repair_completed -> superseded`, the candidate `staged -> open`, installs its first-node cursor, and moves the pointer. Implement a complete `ReplayNodeIdentityV1` bijection from each canonical original Unit ID to exactly one conditional executor Unit ID; all state maps, successor indexes, cursor values, decisions, claims, terminals, consumption, and blocked records use only original IDs. A claim CAS requires cursor at that exact unclaimed node, every predecessor consumed, every successor unclaimed, and no other claimed/recovering/terminal node; first claim also changes pass `open -> draining`. Acquisition/renewal/fence requests use exact no-cache `GET /repos/spoonjoy/spoonjoy-delivery/git/ref/heads/release-ledger`; each canonical body/OID must equal the pass/node attempt's journaled expected protected-ledger head, `issuedAtDateMs` is the strict response Date, and `deadlineMs=issuedAtDateMs+900000`. Renewal requires the same OIDC run/job/attempt, a new durable progress/query receipt, and a Date not after the prior deadline; fencing requires a Date at or after it. Renewal or an authorized append may journal the next expected head for the next request, but a caller-selected, stale, or unjournaled head fails. A node journals no-call/dispatch/idempotency before provider access and reaches `terminal` only on an exact result. Green/no-replay/refresh consumption atomically changes terminal to consumed and advances cursor to the exact successor or complete; response loss reconciles exact node/cursor/pass equality and never re-advances. Containment consumption is the sole failure CAS: it consumes the causal node, blocks the entire unclaimed suffix regardless disposition, sets cursor failed, changes draining to failed, and stores exact `ReplayStageFailedV1` in one transaction; response loss reconciles the entire state/event tuple. For original node 32 through 35 with retained Unit 31 state, a one-way `StagedWorkerCleanupRequiredV1` starts staged cleanup independently of node-specific reconciliation; containment requires both exact terminals, and either uncertainty remains recovering at the causal cursor. Lease recovery changes `claimed -> recovering`, fences the claimant, and resolves exact no-call to `unclaimed` with incremented attempt, exact provider result to `terminal`, or uncertainty to continuing recovery; cursor remains at that node throughout. Unit 60d1c checkpoints; Unit 60c29f installs exact repair closure. Each activated pass must traverse its complete manifest-ordered node set and Unit 60c59 even when zero node is `replay_required`; resolved requires cursor complete plus `ReplayPassConsumedV1`, and failure cannot become consumed.
**Live-stage failure schema implementation**: Implement the exact graph-bound live node set/successor index, candidate-specific `StagedWorkerCleanupAuthorityV1`, one-way `StagedWorkerCleanupRequiredV1`, and generic atomic failure record. Unit 31 freezes separate mutation-broker and read-only-reconciler identities before retained success. An exact non-green Unit 32-35 decision ledger-CASes `available -> cleanup_active` at the current cursor and forbids a green terminal or later claim; staged cleanup and node-specific reconciliation then run independently. Unit 36 claim races from the same parent to `fenced_for_promotion`. The mutation broker re-queries `cleanup_active`, performs exact cleanup, and tears down its own route/token. The separate reconciler re-queries candidate/broker nonreachability and alone calls the Ledger Writer's exact method to append `cleanup_terminal`; it cannot mutate a provider or append anything else. An ordinary causal node can close only after exact node cleanup and zero uncertain provider calls; Unit 32 through Unit 35 additionally require the one-use staged-cleanup terminal whenever Unit 31 retained state exists. Its transaction stores its terminal, changes cleanup authority to `retired`, blocks each unstarted successor, and selects the protected Unit 42a records checkpoint. Unit 36 instead uses the sole `LiveStageContainmentPendingV1` transition to consumed-pending-cleanup with cursor/next action Unit 51 and no blocked suffix; Unit 51's exact cleanup becomes causal `LiveStageFailedV1` and blocks only its own successor index.
**Finalization pre-claim schema implementation**: Implement absent registration, root-timed registered-open lease, optional exact claim-dispatch intent, strict `IntentHeadAnchorV1`, CAS-exclusive close-start, exact claim, and closed reconciliation. Authoritative zero registration plus zero claim validates only `FinalizationPreRegistrationClosedV1` after registration-independent partial cleanup and zero submission/job/key/context/capability reachability. Exact registration derives its immutable deadline from the pre-CAS protected-ledger-ref GitHub Date. Before any claim call the coordinator CAS-appends canonical intent from exact registration head. Only a confirmed response may obtain a strict no-cache exact-intent-head anchor with Date `<=registrationDeadlineMs-5000`, then start claim within `0..5000` monotonic milliseconds; a lost response or failed/late anchor cannot dispatch claim. At or after deadline, registered recovery may CAS close-start from exact registration head against intent, or exact intent head against claim; each fork has one winner. Exact registration plus authoritative zero claim validates only `FinalizationPreClaimClosedV1` after the winning close-start, claim-independent cleanup, and registration capability revocation-or-expiry containment. No Durable Object operation exists before observed claim; neither closure can create submission/relay/validator state or later register/claim.
**Replacement relay schema implementation**: Implement the independently identified operation-scoped replacement relay and monotonic generation CAS. Registered recovery can authorize the next generation only after `dispatch_committed`; it cannot retrieve or append. The relay retrieves the immutable committed candidate, revalidates exact bindings, obtains one narrow generation token, and calls only Ledger Writer exact-candidate append. Response loss is tokenlessly observed; a higher generation requires exact predecessor terminal/uncertainty reconciliation and cannot change bytes, parent, or candidate.
**Final timing schema implementation**: Implement typed `S/C/R` records and response/timeout/transport-error unions. `S` and `C` use the same strict no-cache protected-source-ref contract and frozen OID; `R` exists only for exact provider response. Nonresponse records remain evidence but fail the generation and forbid a repeated provider query. Implement exact order/skew/batch/evidence-age equations and boundary errors.
**Replay response-loss schema implementation**: Store `ReplayNodeConsumedV1` in the same CAS as ordinary cursor advance. Lost-response validation accepts the exact immediate poststate or an immutable receipt whose ordered receipt chain proves the current state is a monotonic descendant under the same pass/manifest; it never repeats consumption.

### ⬜ Unit 5b: Typed Operation DAGs - Implementation
**Release-tag graph**: Implement root-install, local three-GET-only observation, and projection graphs. In one credential-bound process the observer sends `Cache-Control: no-cache` to `GET /user`, the exact delivery collaborator-permission endpoint, and the policy-pinned exact ruleset endpoint; requires three fresh `200`s, exact `16390116`/`arimendelow` user identity, exact collaborator identity with built-in `permission: admin` and `role_name: admin`, complete bypass actors, bound bodies/Date/request IDs, and stable ruleset ETag; then emits one-use governance-key-signed `ProtectedReleaseTagSnapshotV1`. It rejects cache/identity/role/time drift and cannot mutate or call other GETs. Projection binds a fresh pre-snapshot, allows only Ledger Writer broker creation of one pre-existing canonical annotated tag/ref from an exact observed publication, then waits for and consumes a fresh post-snapshot with identical ETag/normalized digest before terminal. Reconcile complete tag/Release equality on retry; implicit tag creation, mismatched existing state, and any update/delete/upload route contain.
**Finalization recovery graphs**: Implement exact-Date validator-lease trigger/reconcile, first-dispatch, independent raw fallback retrieval, sealed-recovery, retrieval-only repair, and other repair DAGs. The trigger carries only identity/nonces into no-egress validation; rollback/commit and stale-attempt fencing prevent partial state. First dispatch returns validated bytes only after commit; persistent primary response failure uses the distinct digest-verifying read-only fallback. If both readers fail, only the root-allowlisted retrieval adapter may change under zero-storage-write proof. Repair brokers deactivate or expiry-contain operation route/version/secrets and prove nonreachability before terminal observation. Frozen semantic/candidate/transition state cannot change.
**Replay execution graph implementation**: Implement one active pointer, sole absent-pointer/zero-history `ReplayPassGenesisV1`, later resolved/repair-completed supersession, complete `ReplayNodeIdentityV1` bijection, `ReplayManifestCursorV1`, exact root-duration lease records, and the pass/node transitions from Unit 2b. Canonical original Unit IDs alone key pass state, cursor, and successor maps; executor IDs are immutable dispatch metadata. Cursor at the first node gates the first claim and opens draining. Every claim requires the exact cursor node, consumed prefix, unclaimed suffix, and no other claimed/recovering/terminal node. Each node journals before provider access and reaches terminal only on exact result. Green/no-replay/refresh consumption advances the cursor atomically; containment consumption atomically consumes the causal node, blocks the full suffix regardless disposition, sets cursor failed, and changes pass to failed. Lease takeover uses a fresh protected-ledger-ref Date, fences the claimant, and resolves only no-call retry, exact result, or continuing uncertainty while retaining the cursor. Unit 60d1c checkpoints; Unit 60c29f installs repair closure. Each activated pass traverses the complete manifest order, including all-green passes, and only cursor complete plus Unit 60c59 exact consumption resolves it.
**What**: Implement `src/operation-graph.ts`, `src/receipts.ts`, deterministic rollback-target selection/setup planning, closed source-agnostic Worker invocation-surface inventory/preflight/containment with `WorkerOverrideBarrierV1`, fixed source-agnostic governance, repository-distinct supervisor-envelope start/provisional-challenge/local-zero/provision-or-abort/rotate/retire graphs, distinct inert Ledger Writer and Delivery Guardian registration/permission-elevation/key-import graphs, root/reviewer/governance signer provision/challenge/retire graphs, reviewer-envelope consumption, Guardian exact-provenance check emission, a post-root delivery-governance/broker-upgrade graph, root-authorized `source-guardian-cutover-v1`, canonical expression resolution, dry-run/revalidation/receipt/containment, and `ledger-root-bootstrap-v1`. The Worker graph requires explicit false source config for preview URLs and `workers.dev`, byte-equal authoritative before/after inventories, and a barrier established before candidate upload: every production route/custom domain has a provider-enforced default-deny for the version-override header; one run-owned staging hostname is protected by one exact Access service-auth application/policy and a claim-bound broker-held provider token; every staged invocation consumes a distinct one-use broker authorization without exposing that token; every service-binding caller strips or rejects the header; and the exact header value/version ID are forbidden from public evidence. Correct-version hostile probes must fail without the broker identity on every external and internal path, while one authorized staging probe succeeds. Capability/order ambiguity, unknown surface, or inability to prove denial before upload requires redesign and a new reviewed graph; it never degrades to version-ID secrecy. Promotion removes the staging exception and revokes the provider token only after the candidate is normal production, while failure removes the candidate from deployment membership before barrier teardown; the permanent production-header denial remains. Key graphs bind Cloudflare secret prestate, provisional/active event generation, canonical X25519 key/fingerprint, exact Started commit, byte-identical repository/generation/key/fingerprint plus challenge/local-zero receipts in Provisioned, exact challenge/ordinary OIDC allowlists and non-consuming wrong-kind branches, active-run retention, bootstrap credential, delete/contain alternatives, and terminal. Identity registration permits only metadata-only/no-webhook/no-key/no-OAuth/no-bypass creation, records distinct provider IDs, and on ambiguity proves exact inert identities or removes them. Root installation is an ordered `rootAttemptGeneration` DAG with separate nodes for one-time journal genesis, later fast-forward attempt starts, each App elevation, each App key import, signer-policy activation, workflow permissions, merged-branch deletion, main rules, ledger rules, environment, tag rules, and ledger genesis. Generation 1 alone creates the absent journal and appends `RootAttemptStarted(1)`; generation N>1 consumes exact `RootAttemptAborted(N-1)` at the journal head and appends `RootAttemptStarted(N)` with byte-equal intent/prestate without replaying genesis. Every node has exact prestate/request/response-loss/post-query/rollback; failure appends `RootAttemptAborted`, increments generation, and cannot continue. Elevation permits only intent-bound Ledger Writer delivery `contents:write` plus `actions:read`/`checks:read`, and Guardian `actions:read`/`contents:read`/`checks:write`. Repository targets are exactly workflow `{read,false}` and `delete_branch_on_merge:true`. Guardian check emission accepts only exact workflow provenance and a canonical valid unconsumed reviewer envelope; it cannot append ledger. Source cutover binds exact root/retirement events, source identities/prestates/targets, journaled resume/rollback/containment, and terminal `SourceGuardianCutoverCompleted`. Post-root upgrade binds exact root event and all live settings/broker prestates, permits reviewed deltas/rollback/disable containment, and forbids root/genesis changes. Only `GENESIS_CREATED` permits `RootAuthorityInstalled`; its direct-child `RootSignerRetired` proves root private-key deletion before source cutover. Candidate selection retains documented total-order/fail-closed rules.
**Live-stage failure graph implementation**: Generate the exact live node set and successor index from the protected Product Change order. Unit 31 retained success also freezes the exact candidate-specific mutation broker, separate read-only cleanup reconciler, and their shared closed cleanup-authority generation. Activation requires an allowed causal non-green node at the current cursor and no later claim; Unit 36's claim edge atomically fences the same authority generation. The broker alone mutates the provider and tears down its own route/token; only after that teardown may the separately credentialless reconciler prove complete candidate/broker nonreachability and invoke the Ledger Writer's exact cleanup-terminal method. Every ordinary member receives one generic failure edge that requires exact terminal cleanup/provider reconciliation and atomically selects the Unit 42a checkpoint with a complete blocked suffix; Unit 32 through Unit 35 additionally require the staged-cleanup terminal whenever retained state exists. Unit 42a, Unit 42b, Unit 42c, Unit 50a, Unit 50b, and Unit 50c remain checkpoint metadata outside the set. Unit 36 receives only the pending-containment bridge to Unit 51; Unit 51 receives the cleanup-to-failure edge that blocks its own suffix.
**Finalization pre-claim graph implementation**: Registration and claim are distinct graph nodes preceded by absent registration and separated by a root-timed registration lease plus optional canonical intent and intent-head anchor. The registration edge has an authoritative zero-registration/zero-claim closure through registration-independent cleanup and `FinalizationPreRegistrationClosedV1`. From exact registration head, confirmed intent alone obtains the strict no-cache exact-head anchor that permits bounded immediate claim launch; intent response loss or anchor failure has no claim edge. At/after deadline, close-start competes with intent from registration head or with claim from intent head. Exact observed claim continues; exact close-start plus authoritative zero claim uses claim-independent cleanup and `FinalizationPreClaimClosedV1`. Closure destroys applicable capability/context/keys, proves no Durable Object operation exists, and routes to Unit 64a1 with `finalization_generation=null`.
**Replacement relay graph implementation**: Pre-register exact service identity/policy and a monotonic generation state machine. Recovery can authorize only the next generation after committed dispatch; the relay can retrieve only exact committed bytes and invoke only exact-candidate Ledger Writer append with one-use authority. Journals and tokenless queries resolve every crash while preserving candidate/parent.
**Final timing graph implementation**: Generate strict two-anchor/provider-response query nodes. `S` and `C` bind the same frozen source ref and GitHub no-cache policy; `R` binds exact provider response. Timeout/error records still obtain `C`, fail closed, and expose no retry edge. Successful records enforce every order/skew/batch/age equation before package assembly.
**Replay descendant graph implementation**: Ordinary cursor advance emits immutable `ReplayNodeConsumedV1` atomically. Recovery validates either immediate poststate or a gap-free receipt path to a monotonic descendant in the same pass/manifest, including a later active node, failed containment, complete/resolved state, or exact supersession lineage; it has no write/retry edge.
**Output**: Validated static DAG and runtime receipt APIs plus `operation dry-run|verify-receipts` CLI.
**Acceptance**: Generic fixtures resolve one authorized branch per node and preserve hash-linked provenance without embedding assumptions about unvalidated source workflows.

**What**: Implement finalization/cleanup/projection libraries, supervisor/recovery CLI, ledger/coordinator/abort-recovery/evidence/projection workflows, and attestations. Coordinator registers recovery then claims. Resolver gates merges/transitions and permits only pre-authorized retrieval-only or transport/provider-adapter repair, never unrelated work. Matching recovery can expire/abort before dispatch, execute exact repair after `dispatch_committed`, authorize a monotonic replacement-relay generation, or emit protected `PublicationObserved` after exact ref observation, but cannot receive package bytes or append publication. Cleanup uses staged receipts, source teardown, isolated `PromotionObservedV1` witness, and distinct claim-bound submitter. One Durable Object transaction atomically stores/seals from `absent_open`; recovery close competes as `absent_open -> closed_before_submission`. Relay starts `unvalidated`. The trigger sends only identity/nonces. Lease claim derives issue/deadline from the exact protected-ledger-ref Date, the attempt's journaled expected head, and root 120000ms duration. Validation transaction reads sealed state, derives strict verdict/candidate, stores immutable validation plus canonical primary and independently encoded raw fallback payloads, and installs `validated_pending_dispatch`; crash rolls back and response loss is observable. First dispatch with fresh `E` commits `dispatch_committed` before primary return. Persistent primary return failure authorizes the next independent replacement relay to use the read-only fallback with digest verification and zero writes. Dual-reader deterministic failure permits only retrieval-adapter changes proved zero-storage-write; other repair excludes parser/scanner/evidence/candidate-builder/DO state-transition paths. Both repair branches execute exact PR, Guardian checks, protected merge, reconciliation, isolated deploy, post-query, rollback/containment, activation, and terminal teardown. Withdrawal is pre-dispatch only; replacement relays preserve package/candidate. No coordinator seal, accepted intermediate, unvalidated dispatch, or post-close submission exists.
**Finalization timing and recovery implementation**: Derive claim and validator-lease times only from strict exact-ref GitHub-Date policy. Implement the two-phase zero-provider-call barrier. Validator service mutations are the root-duration lease CAS and no-egress validation transaction; commit stores independent primary/raw payload representations. Primary post-dispatch response failure routes to read-only fallback, and both-reader failure routes only to zero-storage-write retrieval repair. If any repair activates an isolated broker, publication enters teardown-pending until route/dispatch removal, secret revocation, version deactivation-or-expiry containment, and full nonreachability query complete. Reconcile loss idempotently; only then emit terminal recovery/effective observation.
**Replacement relay authority implementation**: Pre-register an operation-scoped relay service identity/code/policy and monotonic generation state before claim. Matching recovery remains unable to append publication; after `dispatch_committed` it can authorize only the next generation. The relay independently retrieves and revalidates the immutable committed candidate, receives a one-use exact-candidate Ledger Writer token, journals deterministic object/CAS progress, and observes tokenlessly. A higher generation is allowed only after exact predecessor reconciliation and can never change bytes, parent, or candidate.
**Pre-registration finalization implementation**: Reconcile registration append independently. Authoritative zero registration and zero claim after preflight/setup failure or registration CAS loss authorizes only registration-independent Unit 64b cleanup and `FinalizationPreRegistrationClosedV1`; it destroys partial jobs/context/keys, proves zero submission/capability reachability, enters Unit 64a1 with null generation, and cannot later register or claim.
**Pre-claim finalization implementation**: Registration freezes a strict protected-ledger-ref GitHub-Date `FinalizationRegistrationLeaseV1`. Before claim dispatch, CAS canonical `FinalizationClaimDispatchIntentV1` from exact registration head. Only a confirmed append response may obtain strict no-cache `IntentHeadAnchorV1` with exact head/body/OID and Date `<=registrationDeadlineMs-5000`; only that anchor starts claim from exact intent head within `0..5000` monotonic milliseconds. At/after deadline, close-start competes with intent from registration head or with claim from intent head. Reconcile intent, claim, and close responses without a total retry cap; a lost intent response or missing/late anchor cannot dispatch claim. Exact claim presence continues normally and alone permits Durable Object creation. Exact close-start plus authoritative zero claim after no intent, stranded intent, CAS loss, or parent drift cancels/fences frozen jobs, executes only the claim-independent subset of Unit 64b, revokes or expiry-contains registration capability, destroys local context/keys, emits `FinalizationPreClaimClosedV1`, and enters Unit 64a1 with `finalization_generation=null`; Unit 64b1 and claim-dependent mailbox/relay transitions are forbidden.
**Output**: Exact-SHA protected workflow append path and complete Release Set compiler.
**Acceptance**: Local fixture history ships only via `ReleaseSetPublished`; projection retries do not alter ledger truth.

### ⬜ Unit 11b: Delivery CI and Settings Contracts - Implementation
**Required-check authority**: `ProtectedMainV1` does not trust a GitHub Actions check from `{context, Actions App ID}` alone. Its required contexts must be emitted by the distinct Delivery Guardian App after the Guardian broker re-queries and binds exact workflow database ID/path, protected workflow blob SHA, event, head SHA, run/attempt/job, artifact digest, check payload, and a canonical `ReviewerEnvelopeV1` signed by the root-anchored workflow-inaccessible Secure Enclave reviewer key. Branch rules require zero human approvals and zero bypass. `ProtectedLedgerV1` separately permits only the Ledger Writer App bypass.
**Release-tag authority**: Normalize/verify active tag rulesets independently from branch/ledger rules. `ProtectedReleaseTagV1` matches only recursive `refs/tags/release-set/**/*`, proves the canonical two-level tag is included under GitHub `FNM_PATHNAME`, requires creation/update/deletion/non-fast-forward restrictions, and exactly one always-bypass Ledger Writer Integration. Keep Administration permission away from Ledger Writer. A distinct root-authorized local governance observer uses one operator credential/session for only no-cache `GET /user`, exact collaborator-permission GET, and policy-pinned ruleset GET. It requires exact provider-returned actor/collaborator ID/login, built-in `admin` permission/role, three fresh `200`s, bound bodies/Date/request IDs, complete bypass actors, and stable ruleset ETag before signing canonical one-use `ProtectedReleaseTagSnapshotV1`; cache hits, `304`, identity/role/time drift, and any other route fail. Its broker validates `release-tag-project-v1` against fresh matching pre/post snapshots, requires the canonical annotated tag ref already exist before Release POST, creates an exact body-only empty-assets GitHub Release with fixed note/latest/discussion settings, and exposes no update/delete/force/general-tag/release-asset endpoint.
**What**: Add SHA-pinned CI/resolver. Define `ProtectedMainV1` with repository-specific `{context, expected Guardian App ID}` required checks and zero classic/ruleset bypass principals or App allowances, and `ProtectedLedgerV1` with exactly the policy-pinned Ledger Writer App bypass and no ordinary writer path; both normalize/stability-check all layers. Add strict repository-settings schemas/adapters for exact no-cache `GET/PUT /repos/spoonjoy/spoonjoy-delivery/actions/permissions/workflow` and exact no-cache `GET` plus bounded `PATCH /repos/spoonjoy/spoonjoy-delivery`; only `read`/`false` and `delete_branch_on_merge: true` are valid target states. Implement a strict semantic workflow scanner that reads every exact protected-tree workflow blob, rejects duplicate/aliased/expressive permission syntax and `pull_request_target`, resolves explicit top-level and job-level effective permissions plus every same-repository relative reusable-workflow edge from that same exact tree, rejects external/dynamic reusable-workflow calls, and emits canonical `WorkflowTokenPolicyV1` with sorted workflow/job/scope inventories, blob/reusable-closure digests, and a policy digest. Allow only explicit `read` scopes and policy-bound job-specific `id-token: write` or `attestations: write`; reject all repository-mutating writes including `actions`, `checks`, `contents`, `deployments`, `discussions`, `issues`, `packages`, `pages`, `pull-requests`, `repository-projects`, `security-events`, and `statuses`. Journal expected settings prestate, request digest, dispatch uncertainty, repeated GET reconciliation, exact post-state, rollback/containment, complete workflow scan, and root/replay binding. Implement serialized broker identity/actor/transition validation, `ReviewerEnvelopeV1` verification and atomic nonce consumption, source-cutover transition validation, plus distinct web/native envelope-key generations and one-use unwrap. Canonical recipient key is raw RFC 7748 32-byte X25519 u-coordinate in base64url without padding; fingerprint is lowercase-hex SHA-256 of those bytes. Capsule encryption is RFC 9180 base-mode HPKE suite `{KEM=X25519-HKDF-SHA256(0x0020),KDF=HKDF-SHA256(0x0001),AEAD=ChaCha20Poly1305(0x0003)}` with fresh ephemeral KEM key and sequence zero. Strict RFC 8785 envelope is exactly `{version:1,suite,enc,ciphertext,payloadDigest,context}` with 32-byte `enc` and base64url-no-padding fields. `info` is `UTF8("spoonjoy-supervisor-capsule-v1") || 0x00 || RFC8785({repository,workflow,keyGeneration})`; AAD binds owner/repository/workflow/ref/SHA/run/attempt/checkRun/job/environment/claim/generation/nonce/expiry/payloadDigest/signerPublicKey. `payloadDigest` hashes canonical plaintext; claim separately binds final-envelope SHA-256. Reject reused `enc`/nonce/context, caller AEAD nonce, and noncanonical encoding. Normal unwrap requires exact active Provisioned event whose `startedCommit`, generation, canonical key, fingerprint, challenge receipt, and prior local-zero receipt validate against Started/broker state; validate OIDC/JTI, consume the ordinary nonce only after kind/endpoint checks, return signer bytes once, and reject replay/retired/contained. Provisional endpoint requires exact Started event/claim/generation/key/fingerprint/challenge job/nonce, checks capsule kind before nonce consumption, decrypts only fixed challenge once, and cannot unwrap signer capsules. Typed wrong-kind rejection uses disjoint nonce namespaces and consumes neither the challenge nor ordinary capsule. Challenge and local-zero receipts bind start commit/key/fingerprint; only an exactly preserving Provisioned transition containing both receipts activates ordinary unwrap. Timeout/crash deletes binding and appends Aborted or contains all unwraps. App key never leaves broker; envelope private key exists first only in the isolated provisioning context, then broker storage, and local purge/fsync proof must precede Provisioned. Model append state independently from credential lease; pre-mint containment, returned expiry/fingerprint persistence, `finally` revocation `204` or expiry containment, tokenless `H`, fresh-token abort CAS, and `cas_uncertain` persistence remain mandatory.

The local broker verifies artifact/operation inputs and executes only fixed Transporter upload or allowlisted ASC POST/PATCH/skip templates. For upload it verifies the pinned signed Transporter launcher/app/JVM/helper/JAR/library tree, materializes the key exactly once as `0400 private_keys/AuthKey_<id>.p8` on a broker-owned private RAM-backed root, sets sterile broker-owned `HOME`/`CWD`/`TMPDIR`, proves all alternate searched key directories absent or denied, and launches only the allowlisted runtime closure with fixed non-secret `-apiKey`/`-apiIssuer` argv. It records every descendant `exec`/ancestry/library load, refuses any process or tree drift, captures/scans output privately, then terminates the complete closure and destroys/fsync-verifies credential memory, file, sterile directories, and root. The complete verified closure is explicitly inside the trusted mutation TCB; all other processes remain credentialless and sandbox-inaccessible. The broker emits signed receipts and never accepts caller-composed requests or returns a key/JWT/generic lease. The supervisor also holds the claim-bound evidence signer and cleanup lease, applies deny-by-default filesystem sandboxing, blocks untrusted child signer/credential/task-port/ptrace/privilege access, terminates/re-enumerates the UID, destroys/fsync-verifies roots, and signs teardown. Separate no-secret job rejects alternate keys, verifies/uploads receipts, and inventories artifacts/caches. Typed runtime IDs, outgoing capture, revalidation, rollback/containment, cancellation, and unsupported-boundary failure remain exact.
**Output**: Receipt-producing exact-operation ASC mutation broker/TestFlight publisher with separately verified sandbox teardown attestation.
**Acceptance**: Fixture dry-runs resolve exactly one branch per node; shell/Ruby/Swift contracts pass.

### ⬜ Unit 60f3a: Finalization Attempt Cleanup Context
**What**: Before private query, initialize enumerable `0700` root and fsynced WAL `ALLOCATING -> OPEN -> SEALED -> PURGING -> PURGED -> PROMOTED`. Run exact cleanup supervisor outside the secret-bearing writer's UID/session and have it hold the shared cleanup lease on the child's behalf; child receives no lock or signer capability. Independently launch a policy-pinned promotion-witness service under a third UID/session with its own ephemeral Ed25519 key, root read capability, and shared-lock capability but no provider credential, cleanup write, child-control, or supervisor-signing capability. Launch a distinct promotion-submitter service under a fourth UID/session with a separate ephemeral Ed25519 key whose private bytes are generated and retained only in locked service memory and cannot be exported through any API; give it an exact HTTPS allowlist only to the Durable Object `PromotionPackageSubmittedV1` and status routes. It has no root/private/provider/cleanup/witness-key/ledger capability and can receive only the final public package plus detached witness attestation after `PROMOTED`. Bind witness and submitter code/policy/public-key/context digests into final preflight and claim before any private work. Linux uses root-owned sockets with `SO_PEERCRED`, `FD_CLOEXEC`, non-dumpable/no-new-privileges children, read-only root/private tmpfs, seccomp and dedicated cgroups killed empty. macOS uses root launchd sockets with local peer credentials, newly created unprivileged UIDs, denied task-port/ptrace/privilege escalation, deny-by-default `sandbox-exec` profiles, and UID-wide process exhaustion. Unsupported boundary is `BLOCKED_HUMAN`, never clean. Cleanup supervisor exclusively purges/restarts its orphans; witness only reads/signs after acquiring shared lock; submitter signs/submits only after an independently verified witness receipt and otherwise destroys its key.
**Output**: Context; cleanup, witness, and promotion-submitter public-key/code/policy/isolation-profile digests; supervisor/child/witness/submitter UID/session/peer/lease/lock/network proofs; denied capability probes; empty cgroup or UID-process inventories; fsynced WAL/restart evidence; and pending/final/witness/submission fixtures.
**Acceptance**: Active-platform boundaries are executable before writes; child cannot reach signer, unwrap material, host APIs, privilege, lease, out-of-root writes, or surviving descendants; cleanup supervisor/coordinator cannot reach witness or submitter keys; witness cannot mutate cleanup/provider/submission state; submitter cannot inspect private roots or submit before valid `PROMOTED` witness proof. Cancellation/restart converges or yields no receipt. Crash injection leaves discoverable state or non-authoritative `.pending`; no final tombstone or package validates without matching `PROMOTED` plus a later direct-read witness signature, and no mailbox accepts bytes without the claim-bound submitter. Partial setup routes to Unit 64b; no provider mutation.
**Pre-registration failure route**: Because no finalization registration or claim exists yet, partial setup first proves authoritative zero registration and zero claim, then invokes Unit 64b's registration-independent cleanup mode. After zero submission/job/key/context/capability reachability it emits `FinalizationPreRegistrationClosedV1` and enters Unit 64a1 with `finalization_generation=null`; it cannot use `FinalizationPreClaimClosedV1`, Unit 64b1, or `FinalizationAborted`.

**What**: Dispatch the exact protected coordinator after green Unit 60f4a. Dispatch waits for approval. Original may submit, validate, and first-dispatch. Registered recovery may win pre-claim close-start, close before dispatch after claim, reconcile lease, authorize a higher trigger/withdrawal, authorize sealed fallback/retrieval repair or the next replacement-relay generation, execute repair teardown, or observe publication; it cannot receive package bytes or invoke Ledger Writer. Exact winning close-start plus authoritative zero claim selects closure for cause `lease_expired_no_intent`, `intent_without_claim`, `claim_cas_loss`, or `parent_drift`: cancel and fence every frozen source, validator, dispatch, witness, and submitter job; invoke only Unit 64b's claim-independent cleanup mode; revoke registration capability or wait for provider-attested expiry containment; destroy local coordinator context and ephemeral keys; re-query zero claim, zero Durable Object operation/submission, zero live job/key, and zero capability reachability; then emit `FinalizationPreClaimClosedV1` and enter Unit 64a1 with `finalization_generation=null`. **Output**: Exact registration/lease, optional claim-intent and `IntentHeadAnchorV1`, exact claim or close-start commits, `FinalizationPreRegistrationClosedV1`, or `FinalizationPreClaimClosedV1`; strict registration/intent/anchor/claim/close reconciliation; registration, intent, and claim anchor GET/Date/body/request-ID records; registration deadline always and claim/validator deadlines only when claimed; coordinator/recovery/reconciler/fallback-reader/replacement-relay identities; zero-registration-or-zero-claim cancellation/cleanup/revocation/key-destruction/nonreachability proof as applicable; mailbox/barrier/relay/validator lease only when claimed; generations/races; digest-free trigger/dispatch identities; `ValidatorLeaseV1`, recovery, validation, primary/raw payload, fallback retrieval, retrieval-only repair, sealed recovery, other repair, activation, and teardown contracts; witness/submitter/source bindings; actor proof; resolver query.

### ⬜ Unit 63a: Final Attestor Dispatch Plan
**What**: Compile one credential-free plan bound to claim/operation/coordinator/recovery/deadline, `LeaseTimingPolicyV1`, frozen source/validator-trigger/dispatch/fallback-reader jobs, cleanup, nonce, draft/query inventory, witness schema, mailbox, two-phase barrier, close/seal, and `unvalidated` relay. **S/C/R query-clock protocol**: Each Unit 62 query-inventory entry binds one exact protected source repository/ref/OID and no-cache GitHub ref endpoint plus one exact provider method/path. Before credential unwrap or provider access, its source job performs `S`: one bounded `200` GET with canonical body/OID equal to the frozen source SHA, one strict Date, request ID, `Cache-Control: no-cache`, absent/zero `Age`, and no cache-hit indicator. The provider query starts in the same process within `0..5000` monotonic milliseconds after `S` completes. Fresh `E` checks every `T` in `{S,C,R}`: `E.date-T` below `-5000` fails, `[-5000,0]` alone clamps to zero, and normalized age must be `<=360000`.
**CAS timing protocol**: Each write invocation has one epoch with a wall-clock anchor and same-process monotonic offsets. `E`, `F`, and `H` are closed response/timeout/transport-error unions. Same-epoch `H` starts `0..5000` after prior completion, has a 10000ms bound, and adjacent response Dates differ by `-5000..20000`. A replacement epoch first obtains no-cache anchor `A` within 10000, verifies head, then starts `H` within `0..5000` and requires `-5000<=H.date-A.date<=20000`; no monotonic value crosses epochs. Every write token terminates in `finally`; `H` is tokenless; abort CAS uses a fresh journaled token. Atomic/CAS-age claims are forbidden.

Validation atomically stores exact digests/candidate plus canonical primary and raw fallback payloads. First dispatch commits before primary return. Primary serialization/retrieval failure allows recovery to CAS-authorize only the next monotonic `ReplacementRelayGenerationV1`; recovery still receives no bytes and has no append route. The independently identified replacement relay alone calls the fallback, which requires committed state, performs no write, reads raw blobs without the primary serializer, recomputes all bindings, and returns only the exact committed bytes to that same relay. If the first dispatcher dies before append or before observable completion, the same generation rule applies. Other deterministic transport/provider-adapter failure may request the existing protected repair PR/Guardian/merge/reconcile/isolated-deploy/post-query/rollback/activation path. No branch may withdraw, abort, rewrite bytes, rerun sources, alter parent/candidate, or use general governance authority.
**Promotion submission protocol**: `PromotionPackageSubmittedV1` is exact RFC 8785 JSON signed under domain `spoonjoy-promotion-package-submitted-v1` by Unit 61's claim-bound submitter key. It contains only version, operation/claim/generation/coordinator-submission nonce, fresh one-use submission nonce, exact package bytes/digest/length, detached `PromotionObservedV1` bytes/digest/length, witness key/generation/signature digest, expected mailbox state `absent_open`, issuance/expiry, and submitter public-key fingerprint. The only submission route is `POST /operations/<operationID>/promotion-package`; maximum combined body is policy-bound and package remains at most 32768 bytes. In one Durable Object transaction it verifies TLS, strict schema/canonical bytes, claim/head/deadline, both signatures, exact active generation/nonces/digests/lengths, `PROMOTED` binding, and `absent_open` mailbox, then stores the exact byte record directly as immutable `sealed`. Registered recovery alone may call the distinct close route; in one competing transaction it verifies operation/claim/recovery/deadline/abort-required state and changes only `absent_open -> closed_before_submission`, revokes every submission generation/nonce, and records the close proof. There is no coordinator seal endpoint and no `accepted` state. Lost POST or close responses reconcile through exact read-only status. **Relay publication protocol**: `RelayPublicationStateV1` is separate from submission bytes. Only no-egress validation creates `validated_pending_dispatch`; validation and withdrawal serialize. After commit, exact higher `ReplacementRelayGenerationV1` services may retrieve only the same validation bindings through the independent raw fallback and submit only that candidate through the narrow Ledger Writer method; registered recovery merely authorizes the next generation and never receives bytes or append authority. ## Execution

- **TDD strictly enforced**: tests -> red -> implementation -> green -> refactor.
- Commit after each a/b/c phase or atomic live-operation receipt; push every commit.
- Run each repository's focused gate during development and full gate before merge/finalization.
- Before Unit 6c, store every raw transcript and hostile fixture only in private `0600` ephemeral storage and commit no log or transcript. Unit 6c may backfill only scanner-approved public summaries/digests under `./2026-07-20-1958-doing-cross-client-delivery/` after proving repository-history absence, then must purge the retained raw bytes. Later raw evidence remains private ephemeral and is deleted after sanitized proof.
- Before Unit 13b, only the isolated delivery-control-plane mutations explicitly enumerated by Unit 11c1, Unit 12c1, Unit 12c2, Unit 12c3, Unit 12c4, Unit 12c5, Unit 12c6, Unit 12c7, Unit 12c8, Unit 12c9, Unit 12c10, Unit 12c11, Unit 12c12, Unit 12c13, and Unit 12d are allowed. Source edits and source/provider-release mutations are forbidden; the active TestFlight task retains exclusive web/native/production Cloudflare/ASC/TestFlight/build/notification/source-worktree and source-owned broker ownership until protected handoff.
- Provider mutations require authority, dry-run/preflight, protected ledger claim, singleton environment approval, per-request drift checks, receipts, terminal/containment, and post-query.
- The first-install bootstrap family (`github-environment-ui-bootstrap-v1` plus same-target-only resume) and restore-only `github-environment-ui-recovery-v1` are the only operations exempt from their target environment's approval gate: all require protected ledger authorization/claim, authenticated actor-ID proof, bounded UI changes, UI/API evidence, and unambiguous terminal state; none can invoke source/provider mutation code. Bootstrap installs a new gate once; recovery requires proven drift and can only restore the last attested policy. All policy changes use the installed gate.
- Cross-provider publication uses exact, age-bounded, source-owned point-in-time attestations plus a durable protected coordinator; it must never claim an atomic distributed snapshot or lock across Cloudflare and ASC because those control planes expose no shared transaction. Later external changes create new state and require a new Product Change.
- Root-policy-governed `ReplayPassGenesisV1` atomically creates/opens the sole first p1 from absent pointer and zero history with `ReplayManifestCursorV1` at the first original ID; subsequent passes use resolved/repair-completed supersession and a fresh first-node cursor. `ReplayNodeIdentityV1` maps each original Unit ID to one executor, but original IDs alone key replay state and cursor. Each `ReplayNodeSetV1` member claims only the exact cursor node through root-duration `ReplayNodeClaimV1` after proving a consumed prefix, unclaimed suffix, and no other claimed/recovering/terminal node; it derives issue/renew/fence from exact protected-ledger-ref GitHub Dates, journals pre-dispatch/idempotency state, and consumes once. Recovery fences old claimant and reconciles no-call retry, exact success, or containment while cursor remains fixed; uncertainty remains recovering. Green/no-replay/refresh consumption advances cursor atomically. Ordinary containment consumption atomically consumes the causal node, blocks its complete suffix, fails cursor/pass, and emits failed/blocked records. When replay Unit 31 retains staged state, original nodes 32 through 35 must finish node-specific cleanup and the exact generation-bound `StagedWorkerCleanupAuthorityV1` terminal before containment consumption; cleanup uncertainty remains recovering at the causal cursor. Unit 36 is the sole bridge: its non-green terminal atomically consumes node 36 into `ReplayContainmentPendingV1`, advances cursor only to unclaimed node 51, and leaves the later suffix unclaimed; exact Unit 51 cleanup then consumes causal node 51, blocks only its suffix, and fails cursor/pass. Unit 60d1c checkpoints, and exact repair closure alone permits supersession. Each activated pass traverses its full manifest order and Unit 60c59 even when all decisions are no-replay; only cursor complete can resolve and reach final review. A replay inherits original acceptance. Unit 36 containment follows Unit 51, then Unit 42a, Unit 42b, Unit 42c, and Unit 60a. Containment, blocker, failed postcondition, or missing evidence blocks Unit 60d and Unit 61. Renewable age triggers Unit 60c59b read-only staging only.
- `LiveStageNodeSetV1` is the exact ordered live sequence Unit 31, Unit 32, Unit 33, Unit 34, Unit 35, Unit 36, Unit 51, Unit 37, Unit 37a, Unit 38, Unit 39, Unit 40, Unit 41, Unit 42, Unit 43, Unit 44, Unit 45, Unit 46, Unit 47, Unit 48, Unit 49, Unit 50, Unit 52, Unit 53, Unit 54, Unit 54a, Unit 55, Unit 56, Unit 57, Unit 58a, Unit 58b, and Unit 59. Unit 31 retained success freezes one candidate-specific `StagedWorkerCleanupAuthorityV1`, its provider-mutation broker, and a separate credentialless read-only reconciler. An ordinary causal failure completes node-specific cleanup/provider reconciliation before atomic `LiveStageFailedV1` blocks its successor index and enters Unit 42a; Unit 32 through Unit 35 additionally require broker teardown plus the reconciler's exact candidate/broker nonreachability terminal before blocking Unit 51. Unit 36 instead records `LiveStageContainmentPendingV1` and selects executable Unit 51 without blocking it; Unit 51 cleanup then emits causal Unit 51 `LiveStageFailedV1` and blocks only `LiveStageSuccessorIndexV1["51"]`. Unit 36 or Unit 51 disposition retires the staged cleanup authority. The protected Unit 42a, Unit 42b, Unit 42c checkpoint then enters Unit 60a. No live member may terminate merely by saying shipment is blocked, no mutation broker may attest its own cleanup, no retained staged resource may lose an executable cleanup owner, and no blocked successor may call a provider.
- Finalization registration and claim are separate protected transitions. Registration freezes `FinalizationRegistrationLeaseV1` from a strict no-cache protected-ledger-ref GitHub Date. The coordinator appends canonical `FinalizationClaimDispatchIntentV1` from exact registration head; only a confirmed response may obtain a strict no-cache exact-intent-head anchor whose Date is `<=registrationDeadlineMs-5000`, and that anchor alone launches claim within `0..5000` monotonic milliseconds. Exact claim alone permits Durable Object operation creation; exact close-start plus zero claim permits only claim-independent cleanup and `FinalizationPreClaimClosedV1` with null generation.
- Every ordinary replay consumption CAS stores immutable `ReplayNodeConsumedV1` with pass/original-node/attempt/decision/result/manifest/pre-post-cursor/transition/ledger bindings. Immediate-poststate equality is sufficient but not necessary: an exact receipt plus a gap-free same-pass same-manifest receipt chain may prove a monotonic descendant current state through later node activity, containment, completion, resolution, or supersession. Recovery never advances a receipted node twice, and pass consumption requires the complete ordered receipt chain.
- Public-safe records are committed on the named iteration branch and merge through protected PR/CI checkpoints after Unit 29e, Unit 42c, Unit 50c, Unit 59c, and Unit 60f3. A generic live-failure checkpoint may invoke Unit 42c from the active live records branch before its ordinary checkpoint. Finalization consumes only Unit 60f3's exact protected-main commit and GitHub attestation; branch-only records are never authoritative.
- Visual changes or consuming-surface proof require `visual-qa-dogfood` and a closed absurdity ledger.
- Fix ordinary blockers with fresh sub-agents and TDD; surface only true human-only credentials, hardware, or account capability once safe authenticated paths are exhausted.
- Keep checklists and ledger/Desk ownership current after every transition.
